Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the fundamental_group structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 539. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 657 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to fundamental_group theory, we decompose the problem into 539 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 657. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
